# Epidemic Engine production module dossiers

Generated by `docs/freeze/module_dossiers.py` from the current production CMake targets and public headers.

Callable names are a conservative lexical coverage index. Repeated names are shown with occurrence counts and must be resolved to exact overload contracts during the local audit.

### EngineBase/Diagnostics

<!-- module: EngineBase/Diagnostics -->

Target: `EpidemicDiagnostics`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Provides best-effort observational logging, saturating diagnostic counters, scope profiling, and thread-local diagnostic names. It is not a gameplay/event transport and owns no authoritative engine/game state.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] `console_logger.h` (`ConsoleLogger`); `counters.h` (`CounterId`, `DiagnosticsCounters`); `logger.h` (`LogLevel`, `LogMessage`, `ILogger` plus logging enable state); `profiling.h` (`ProfileEvent`, `IProfileCollector`, `InMemoryProfileCollector`, `ProfileScope`); `thread_context.h` (thread-name functions).

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Only `EpidemicFoundation` is linked. Diagnostics has no dependency on Core, Platform, Runtime, or Framework.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] `ILogger` contains concrete logging sink failures behind the private virtual `Write` boundary; `IProfileCollector` is the profiling sink boundary and `ProfileScope` contains collector exceptions.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] No authoritative engine/game state. Module-owned diagnostic-only state consists of atomic counters, logging/profiling enable flags, the current profile collector reference, `InMemoryProfileCollector` samples, `ConsoleLogger` file state, and per-thread diagnostic names.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] No indexes or caches participate in engine correctness. `InMemoryProfileCollector` event storage and `ConsoleLogger` file initialization state are diagnostic-only observations.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] `CounterId` is a closed diagnostic slot enum with `Count` as an invalid sentinel. No generations, revisions, or cursors are owned by the module.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] No engine lifecycle state machine. Logging/profiling enable flags are independent boolean observation gates; a `ProfileScope` captures its collector once at construction and emits at most once on destruction.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] All Diagnostics state is transient and process-local. Counters, collector samples, enable flags and thread names are not save-game or engine-persistence state. `ConsoleLogger` may append text to a log file, which is diagnostic output, not restorable authoritative state.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] No persistence snapshot/restore contract. `InMemoryProfileCollector::Snapshot()` is a detached read copy of diagnostic samples and is not a restore-capable persistence snapshot.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Counters and enable flags are atomic; collector registration is mutex-protected; `InMemoryProfileCollector` and `ConsoleLogger` serialize their internal containers/output; thread names are `thread_local` and returned by owning copy. `ILogger::Log` and `ProfileScope` are exception-containment boundaries for sink failures.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed exact callable inventory is the EngineBase/Diagnostics section of `docs/freeze/public_api_inventory.md`. Mutations are observational only: counter arithmetic/settings, logging/profiling gates and sinks, collector sample storage/reset, and the current thread diagnostic name.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] `CounterCount`, `IsValidCounterId`, counter `Get`, `GlobalCounters`, enum `ToString`, `IsLoggingEnabled`, collector `Snapshot`/`GetProfileCollector`/`IsProfilingEnabled`, and owning `GetCurrentThreadName` expose diagnostic invariants without borrowing mutable module storage.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Diagnostics never owns authoritative engine state; disabled observation is a semantic no-op; logger/collector failures cannot escape the safe RAII/logging boundaries; invalid counter IDs never index storage; counter arithmetic saturates; thread diagnostic names do not escape as borrowed references.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] `CounterId::Count` fixes the counter array size. Counter operations are O(1) lock-free/atomic operations subject to platform atomic implementation; collector and logger operations are O(1) amortized append/write plus sink cost. Counter arithmetic saturates at `INT64_MIN/MAX` instead of wrapping.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Defines dependency-free errors, results, typed IDs/handles, paths, hashes, time/frame values and validation primitives.

### EngineBase/RHI

<!-- module: EngineBase/RHI -->

Target: `EpidemicRHI`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Defines backend-neutral rendering device, context, swap-chain and frame lifecycle contracts, including a lifecycle-strict null backend.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed public surface comprises descriptors/validation, IRhiDevice, IRhiCommandContext, IRhiSwapChain, Null factory, RhiPixelFormat and PresentationSurfaceHandle.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed direct production dependency: EpidemicFoundation only.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed public abstraction ports are IRhiDevice, IRhiCommandContext and IRhiSwapChain. The Null implementation invokes no external callback/provider/backend; D3D11 is a separate module.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed authoritative transient state is limited to NullRhiDeviceState weak active-presentation binding, each NullRhiSwapChain descriptor, each NullRhiCommandContext frame-active flag, and PresentationSurfaceHandle pointer value. There is no upper-layer/gameplay state.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no derived cache or secondary index state.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no ID/generation/revision/cursor space. PresentationSurfaceHandle is an opaque borrowed native pointer wrapper, not an engine identity.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed logical state machine is IRhiCommandContext inactive -> active -> inactive. NullRhiDeviceState is a presentation-binding record, not an independent lifecycle state machine.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: all RHI state is transient. No save-game/persistent state exists in EngineBase/RHI.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no public snapshot/restore contract; RHI state is transient and reconstructed by composition.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed for Goal 2: no internal/public concurrency guarantee; mutable operations require external serialization until Goal 6.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed exact mutators: BeginFrame, Clear, EndFrame, CreateCommandContext, CreateSwapChain, Present and Resize.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed queries/factories cover descriptor validation, frame state, device descriptor/backend name, swap-chain dimensions/format/buffer count, null-device factory, pixel-format names and presentation-handle value/validity/equality.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: command frame is inactive or active; Clear requires active frame plus live presentation target; swap-chain dimensions are non-zero and update atomically; failed replacement/resize preserves prior usable state.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no module-specific budgets/capacities. Baseline operations are constant-state operations; backend work is outside the Null implementation.

### EngineBase/RHI_D3D11

<!-- module: EngineBase/RHI_D3D11 -->

Target: `EpidemicRHI_D3D11`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Implements backend-neutral RHI contracts with D3D11/DXGI while owning all native COM resources and translating native failures into controlled engine errors.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed public surface is one factory header exporting CreateD3D11RhiDevice and no concrete D3D11 class.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed direct production dependencies: EpidemicFoundation, EpidemicRHI, EpidemicPlatform, d3d11 and dxgi.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed external boundary is Win32/D3D11/DXGI. Native failures are translated to Result errors; no upper-layer callback/provider is invoked.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed transient authoritative state: shared D3D11DeviceState owns device/context COM references, feature level, weak active swap-chain binding and device-loss state; each swap chain owns DXGI swap chain/back buffer/RTV/descriptor/recreate flag; each context owns frame-active state.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: active_swap_chain is a weak presentation binding; no secondary cache/index duplicates authoritative gameplay state.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no module-specific IDs, generations, revisions or cursors.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: command frame inactive/active lifecycle comes from RHI; backend adds swap-chain ready/recreate-required and device available/device-lost states.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: all D3D11 backend state is transient and reconstructed by composition. No save-game persistence exists.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no snapshot/restore contract; D3D11 state is transient and must be recreated after process/session restore.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed for Goal 2: mutable D3D11 operations require external serialization until Goal 6 qualifies concurrency.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no module-specific public mutator class; the single factory creates an IRhiDevice. Returned objects implement the already-frozen EngineBase/RHI contracts.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed exact public callable: 37b182fdc4b11948 CreateD3D11RhiDevice. BackendName/Descriptor and swap-chain/context queries are inherited RHI interface contracts.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: native COM ownership is RAII; active target is published only after successful creation; zero-area resize never reaches DXGI; missing RTV is recreate-required; device-loss is terminal for new native work.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed: no module-specific budget. Swap-chain buffer_count/dimensions are descriptor bounded; each operation performs bounded D3D11/DXGI calls.

### EngineBase/Support

<!-- module: EngineBase/Support -->

Target: `EpidemicEngineBaseSupport`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Composes EngineBase service bundles and frame wiring for headless, platform, input and graphics configurations.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] `EngineBase/Support/include/Epidemic/EngineBase/engine_base_support.h` (`EngineBaseOptions`, `GraphicsRuntimeOptions`, `GraphicsRuntimeServices`, `FramePlatformEvents`) and `graphics_backend.h` (`GraphicsBackend`). Forward-declared `ILogger` is an external Diagnostics interface, not a Support-owned type.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Direct target dependencies: `EpidemicFoundation`, `EpidemicCore`, `EpidemicMemory`, `EpidemicDiagnostics`, `EpidemicPlatform`, `EpidemicInput`, `EpidemicRHI`, and private backend dependency `EpidemicRHI_D3D11`.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Composition targets Core `Application`/`ServiceContainer`, Platform interfaces, Input interface, RHI interfaces and the D3D11/Null factories. `GraphicsBackend` selects only Null or D3D11. Frame callbacks are registered into the caller-owned Application.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] No independent authoritative store. Support mutates the caller-owned Application composition graph and registers frame callbacks; authoritative service/runtime state remains owned by the registered Core, Platform, Input and RHI service objects.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] No module-owned cache or index. `FramePlatformEvents` is application-scoped transient handoff storage between platform and input phases.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] None.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] No Support-owned state machine. Registration is permitted only while the caller-owned ServiceContainer is unsealed; application/service lifecycles are owned by Core and the concrete services.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] No persistent state. `FramePlatformEvents` and registered frame callbacks are transient Application composition state; backend device/context/window/swap-chain state is owned by the corresponding service implementations.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] N/A. Support owns no persistent state and exposes no snapshot/restore API.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Composition mutation is owner-thread/external-serialization only. Support does not promise concurrent helper invocation. Registered services retain their own threading contracts.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] A helper never replaces an existing service owner. Multi-service bundles publish all roles together or leave the service map unchanged. Null and D3D11 graphics publish the same IRhiDevice/IRhiCommandContext interface roles. Frame wiring order is Platform, Input, Begin/Clear, End, Present.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Registration work is bounded by the small service bundle size except `RegisterInstancesAtomic`, whose candidate-map copy is O(number of registered service types). `worker_count` must fit signed 64-bit configuration storage; default window dimensions must be positive signed-32-bit values.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] RegionId/SurfaceId registration ownership is stable; global/per-region revisions are uint64 and revision exhaustion is rejected.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Registration has a one-way freeze. Existing registered region/surface values remain mutable after freeze; new identities are forbidden.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] EnvironmentSnapshot is detached observation only; no local persistence restore contract belongs to Goal 3.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Snapshots/projections are observations; no Environment restore/checkpoint API exists in Goal 3.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No synchronization primitive or concurrent-call guarantee is exposed; policy callback execution is synchronous.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Weather/season/climate/surface queries, revisions, snapshots and projections expose invariant state.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Finite numeric domains, canonical wind, immutable surface-to-region ownership, registration freeze and atomic batch publication are enforced.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] No explicit runtime budget/capacity. Batch work is bounded by caller update size; revision exhaustion is checked before commit.

### EngineRuntime/Physics

<!-- module: EngineRuntime/Physics -->

Target: `EpidemicRuntimePhysics`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns collision-shape/body identity, fixed-step timing, backend synchronization, contact/query publication and retryable transform projection through neutral backend/source/sink interfaces.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current public surface: 54 exact callables, each with Block C contract/test anchor in EngineRuntime/Physics/docs/Goal3_Block_C_Evidence_2026-09-21.md.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed module CMake linkage, public dependency carriers and implementation includes; no forbidden peer-major ownership dependency introduced.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Backend creation/simulation/query/contact and transform source/sink failure are contained. Accepted fixed-step prefixes remain committed; sync/projection retry does not resimulate an already accepted backend step. Publication rollback retains cleanup ownership when rollback itself cannot finish.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Authoritative state is registered shapes, body records/generations, backend-handle ownership, accumulator/fixed-step counters, contact buffer, pending backend cleanup and pending transform projections.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed derived indexes/work lists and private pending/reconciliation records against rollback and deterministic-order regressions.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Body IDs/generations, backend handle uniqueness, revisions, accumulator/time counters and max_substeps are checked. Raycast normalization is overflow-safe for large finite float directions.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Backend initialization and Runtime Shutdown are explicit. Shutdown cleans bodies before referenced shapes, continues independent cleanup best-effort and retries unfinished backend destruction without repeating completed cleanup.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Physics exposes no module-local snapshot/restore surface; physical Runtime state is transient and reconstructed by higher-level composition.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Physics exposes no module-local snapshot/restore surface; physical Runtime state is transient and reconstructed by higher-level composition.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger thread-affinity guarantee is inferred; deterministic module-local ownership and external interface boundaries are preserved.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] All generated mutation/lifecycle child obligations are individually REVIEWED; see EngineRuntime/Physics/docs/Goal3_Block_C_Evidence_2026-09-21.md Mutator obligation review.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed query/value surfaces against module regressions for detached/state-consistent output and invalid/stale inputs.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed accepted-work ownership, failure atomicity, identity/lifecycle boundaries and permanent defect regressions.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Body IDs/generations, backend handle uniqueness, revisions, accumulator/time counters and max_substeps are checked. Raycast normalization is overflow-safe for large finite float directions.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No synchronization primitive or concurrent-call guarantee is exposed; tested behavior is synchronous.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Node/parent/children, transform/bounds, dirty state, queries, revision and snapshot APIs provide invariant observation.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Acyclic hierarchy, valid parent ownership, finite/non-degenerate TRS, representable subtree world state and valid bounds are pre-commit invariants.

### EngineRuntime/Simulation

<!-- module: EngineRuntime/Simulation -->

Target: `EpidemicRuntimeSimulation`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns simulation jobs, scheduled tasks, world-memory events, attention/relevance state, abstract facts and proposal queue infrastructure; downstream domain state remains external.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current public surface: 55 exact callables, each with Block C contract/test anchor in EngineRuntime/Simulation/docs/Goal3_Block_C_Evidence_2026-09-21.md.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed module CMake linkage, public dependency carriers and implementation includes; no forbidden peer-major ownership dependency introduced.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Callback Result failure/exception is contained. A successful ExecuteStep is accepted once and retained across later local staging failure. Named allocation seams prove attention/proposal/work-list failures do not publish partial state.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Authoritative state is jobs/generations, task schedule, memory events, attention maps, facts/revision, proposal batches and shutdown state. Successful ExecuteStep result may be retained as private pending accepted state until publication completes.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed derived indexes/work lists and private pending/reconciliation records against rollback and deterministic-order regressions.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Job/work budgets, terminal-job/memory/fact/proposal capacities, generated IDs, generations and fact revision have checked boundary behavior.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Shutdown becomes terminal for new Tick/Schedule/proposal publication as soon as cleanup starts; cancellation/cleanup remain retryable and completed shutdown is idempotent.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] No module-local snapshot/restore API exists in Goal 3. Whole-engine persistence and ordered restore remain composition/system responsibilities.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] No module-local snapshot/restore API exists in Goal 3. Whole-engine persistence and ordered restore remain composition/system responsibilities.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger thread-affinity guarantee is inferred; deterministic module-local ownership and external interface boundaries are preserved.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] All generated mutation/lifecycle child obligations are individually REVIEWED; see EngineRuntime/Simulation/docs/Goal3_Block_C_Evidence_2026-09-21.md Mutator obligation review.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed query/value surfaces against module regressions for detached/state-consistent output and invalid/stale inputs.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed accepted-work ownership, failure atomicity, identity/lifecycle boundaries and permanent defect regressions.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Job/work budgets, terminal-job/memory/fact/proposal capacities, generated IDs, generations and fact revision have checked boundary behavior.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed derived indexes/work lists and private pending/reconciliation records against rollback and deterministic-order regressions.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] No module-local snapshot/restore API exists. Persistent world/resource data stays behind dependency ports; retry state is transient Runtime ownership.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] No module-local snapshot/restore API exists. Persistent world/resource data stays behind dependency ports; retry state is transient Runtime ownership.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger thread-affinity guarantee is inferred; deterministic module-local ownership and external interface boundaries are preserved.

### EngineRuntime/Support

<!-- module: EngineRuntime/Support -->

Target: `EpidemicRuntimeSupport`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Official Runtime composition/cross-major adapter layer; no duplicate gameplay/world ownership.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Public Support surface is runtime_support.h; no Framework type dependency is introduced into EngineRuntime.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Support is the allowed cross-major Runtime composition layer and links Runtime majors plus Core/Foundation; majors do not link back to Support.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Production dependencies and event/resource/backend callbacks are explicit typed ports; Result/exception containment reviewed at adapter/coordinator boundaries.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns composition root, adapter ownership, coordinator pending-frame/shutdown bookkeeping and reference-adapter local ownership only.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Pose dual indexes, prepared streaming data, lease tables and reference event batch are derived/integration state with explicit strong-guarantee publication.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Support does not create a second global identity authority; local reference IDs/cursors commit only with authoritative publication.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Coordinator active -> shutdown-started -> complete, pending-frame retry, adapter cleanup and projection/lease ownership transitions reviewed.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Support has no save-authoritative persistent state; composition/adapters/pending frame/shutdown cursors are transient runtime state.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] N/A for module-level persistence snapshot/restore; Support exposes detached integration observations and private retry state instead.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No public multi-thread mutation guarantee is claimed; composition/coordinator mutation is owner-thread orchestration and external callbacks are contained.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Register/Prepare/Commit/Tick/Shutdown and typed adapter mutations reviewed for precondition, success and failure atomicity.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Orders, shutdown flags, pose/prepared-data/projection queries return explicit observations without becoming second authoritative state.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] One adapter object per advertised role set, accepted-prefix ownership, no lost lease/projection/event ownership, terminal coordinator semantics.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Fixed 12-step update/shutdown sequences, coordinator reserved bookkeeping, per-major budgets and bounded adapter queues/ledgers are explicit.

### EngineRuntime/Time

<!-- module: EngineRuntime/Time -->

Target: `EpidemicRuntimeTime`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Sole Runtime owner of authoritative game clock progression, scale, pause state, deterministic fractional remainder and time events.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] TimeCheckpoint is persistence-complete; TimeSnapshot remains frame-facing observation.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] RuntimeFoundation and Foundation only; no Runtime peer dependency.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] None.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] now, normalized time scale, pause state, fractional remainder and revision.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] TimeSnapshot and calendar/day-phase observations are rebuilt from candidate authoritative state; no secondary lookup index.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] No handle ID space; revision is checked and exhaustion rejects before mutation.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Running/paused semantics are explicit; pause/resume duplicate operations are no-ops.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Checkpoint includes remainder/scale/pause/revision/config identity; last_delta/events/operation-only state are transient.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Validate complete checkpoint and compatibility, build candidate off-state, one no-fail commit, clear transient state.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No internal worker/thread ownership; synchronous caller-serialized mutable runtime contract.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Advance, Pause, Resume, SetTimeScale, Skip and RestoreCheckpoint; all have candidate-before-commit or explicit no-op behavior.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Now, LastDelta, GetSnapshot and CaptureCheckpoint expose deterministic state observations.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Non-negative time, normalized positive scale, canonical phase boundaries, rational remainder bounds and deterministic event order.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] All calendar/scaling multiplications and revision/time bounds checked before commit; event staging reserves before publication.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/AI

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/AI -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkAI`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns AI profiles/goals/considerations, agents, blackboards, intents, think scheduling indexes, generators, revisions and the bounded AI change journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 90 exact callable rows. Contract/test anchors are in `_goal4_handoff/B05/public_api_anchors.json` and `docs/EngineFramework/AI.md`.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation public dependencies. B05 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] AI evaluators and target/input providers are decision inputs only. Throws/failures are isolated before authoritative agent/intent/schedule publication, with deterministic tie breaking retained.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns AI profiles/goals/considerations, agents, blackboards, intents, think scheduling indexes, generators, revisions and the bounded AI change journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Derived indexes and bounded journal/read models were reviewed with primary state. Fault paths may not publish a mismatched derived view.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Generators, revisions and change sequence/cursor state are explicit contract boundaries. Exhaustion is rejected before publication and restore validates continuity.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle states, allowed transitions, duplicate/terminal/retry behavior and wrong-lifecycle failures were reviewed against module tests.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative semantic records and continuity state required by the module. Transient provider/executor bindings are not reused as persistent semantic identities.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates into off-state and keeps the live service unchanged on validation/allocation failure; successful restore publishes a self-consistent state and advances/reset journal epoch according to contract.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Externally serialized/owner-thread for Goal 4 local correctness. No new thread-safety promise is introduced; Goal 6 owns concurrency qualification.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 104 mutation obligations across success, no-op, invalid/precondition and failure semantics.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were checked against authoritative records and deterministic derived indexes/order; diagnostic state is not promoted to authority.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, indexes, generators, revisions, lifecycle and journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Numeric bounds, budgets/retention and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit. B05 fixes preserve bounded journal/summary behavior and avoid unchecked signed overflow.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Economy

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Economy -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkEconomy`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns currencies, accounts, fund reservations, offers, trades, debts, contracts, economy revisions/generators and the economy journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 105 exact admission callables. Per-callable contract and test anchors are in _goal4_handoff/B03/public_api_anchors.json and docs/EngineFramework/Economy.md.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation and module-specific public dependencies. B03 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Price/provider failures are explicit service inputs. Admission emitted no separate external-boundary candidate rows. Callback/provider failures are converted to Result/error semantics; durable reconciliation is retained where rollback can fail.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns currencies, accounts, fund reservations, offers, trades, debts, contracts, economy revisions/generators and the economy journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Secondary indexes, prepared/pending records and bounded journal state were reviewed against primary records; failure paths do not intentionally publish partial index state.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] ID generators, revision and journal sequence/cursor state are persistent contract state where exposed. Publication is staged so failed operations do not consume IDs or revisions.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle candidates were reviewed for allowed, forbidden, terminal, cancel/release/consume/reconciliation and retry behavior as applicable to the module.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative persistent records and required generator/revision/journal continuity. Provider/runtime handles that are only transient are not promoted to reusable semantic identity.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates and stages candidate state before publication; B03 private restore fault seams verify failed staging leaves the live state and persistent counters unchanged where allocation evidence is required.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger internal thread-safety guarantee is asserted. B03 local correctness assumes externally serialized/owner-thread access; engine-wide concurrency qualification remains Goal 6.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 108 generated mutation obligations across success, no-op, preconditions/invalid input and failure semantics in coverage_reviews.json.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were reviewed against authoritative state, stale/invalid identities and deterministic index-derived results in the registered module suite.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, secondary indexes, reservation/transaction state and revision/journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Capacity/quantity/time/counter bounds and journal retention are reviewed; integer overflow and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit failure boundaries.

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkKnowledge`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns knowledge profile definitions, knowledge/memory records, relations/contradictions, indexes, generators, revisions and the bounded knowledge change journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 40 exact callable rows. Contract/test anchors are in `_goal4_handoff/B05/public_api_anchors.json` and `docs/EngineFramework/Knowledge.md`.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation public dependencies. B05 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] The admission inventory contains the block’s single external-boundary candidate in Knowledge. Provider/callback failure is reviewed as a local Result/error boundary and may not partially publish graph/index state.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns knowledge profile definitions, knowledge/memory records, relations/contradictions, indexes, generators, revisions and the bounded knowledge change journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Derived indexes and bounded journal/read models were reviewed with primary state. Fault paths may not publish a mismatched derived view.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Generators, revisions and change sequence/cursor state are explicit contract boundaries. Exhaustion is rejected before publication and restore validates continuity.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle states, allowed transitions, duplicate/terminal/retry behavior and wrong-lifecycle failures were reviewed against module tests.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative semantic records and continuity state required by the module. Transient provider/executor bindings are not reused as persistent semantic identities.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates into off-state and keeps the live service unchanged on validation/allocation failure; successful restore publishes a self-consistent state and advances/reset journal epoch according to contract.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Externally serialized/owner-thread for Goal 4 local correctness. No new thread-safety promise is introduced; Goal 6 owns concurrency qualification.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 48 mutation obligations across success, no-op, invalid/precondition and failure semantics.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were checked against authoritative records and deterministic derived indexes/order; diagnostic state is not promoted to authority.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, indexes, generators, revisions, lifecycle and journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Numeric bounds, budgets/retention and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit. B05 fixes preserve bounded journal/summary behavior and avoid unchecked signed overflow.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Loot

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Loot -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkLoot`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns loot tables, generated rewards, pending delivery records, delivery lifecycle, explicit random-source results, revisions/generators and the loot journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 52 exact admission callables. Per-callable contract and test anchors are in _goal4_handoff/B03/public_api_anchors.json and docs/EngineFramework/Loot.md.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation and module-specific public dependencies. B03 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Reward handler prepare/commit/cancel and reconciliation are explicit ports. Admission emitted no separate external-boundary candidate rows. Callback/provider failures are converted to Result/error semantics; durable reconciliation is retained where rollback can fail.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns loot tables, generated rewards, pending delivery records, delivery lifecycle, explicit random-source results, revisions/generators and the loot journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Secondary indexes, prepared/pending records and bounded journal state were reviewed against primary records; failure paths do not intentionally publish partial index state.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] ID generators, revision and journal sequence/cursor state are persistent contract state where exposed. Publication is staged so failed operations do not consume IDs or revisions.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle candidates were reviewed for allowed, forbidden, terminal, cancel/release/consume/reconciliation and retry behavior as applicable to the module.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative persistent records and required generator/revision/journal continuity. Provider/runtime handles that are only transient are not promoted to reusable semantic identity.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates and stages candidate state before publication; B03 private restore fault seams verify failed staging leaves the live state and persistent counters unchanged where allocation evidence is required.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger internal thread-safety guarantee is asserted. B03 local correctness assumes externally serialized/owner-thread access; engine-wide concurrency qualification remains Goal 6.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 64 generated mutation obligations across success, no-op, preconditions/invalid input and failure semantics in coverage_reviews.json.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were reviewed against authoritative state, stale/invalid identities and deterministic index-derived results in the registered module suite.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, secondary indexes, reservation/transaction state and revision/journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Capacity/quantity/time/counter bounds and journal retention are reviewed; integer overflow and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit failure boundaries.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/NavigationSemantics -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkNavigationSemantics`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns semantic navigation profiles, layers and links/rules, their indexes, layer identity generator, revisions and the bounded navigation-semantics change journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 69 exact callable rows. Contract/test anchors are in `_goal4_handoff/B05/public_api_anchors.json` and `docs/EngineFramework/NavigationSemantics.md`.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation public dependencies. B05 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Capability lookup/evaluation is a semantic provider boundary. Failure is not authoritative navigation state and may not leave a partial layer/link/index publication.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns semantic navigation profiles, layers and links/rules, their indexes, layer identity generator, revisions and the bounded navigation-semantics change journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Derived indexes and bounded journal/read models were reviewed with primary state. Fault paths may not publish a mismatched derived view.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Generators, revisions and change sequence/cursor state are explicit contract boundaries. Exhaustion is rejected before publication and restore validates continuity.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle states, allowed transitions, duplicate/terminal/retry behavior and wrong-lifecycle failures were reviewed against module tests.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative semantic records and continuity state required by the module. Transient provider/executor bindings are not reused as persistent semantic identities.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates into off-state and keeps the live service unchanged on validation/allocation failure; successful restore publishes a self-consistent state and advances/reset journal epoch according to contract.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Externally serialized/owner-thread for Goal 4 local correctness. No new thread-safety promise is introduced; Goal 6 owns concurrency qualification.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 60 mutation obligations across success, no-op, invalid/precondition and failure semantics.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were checked against authoritative records and deterministic derived indexes/order; diagnostic state is not promoted to authority.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, indexes, generators, revisions, lifecycle and journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Numeric bounds, budgets/retention and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit. B05 fixes preserve bounded journal/summary behavior and avoid unchecked signed overflow.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Processes

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Processes -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkProcesses`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns process definitions, recipes, stations, process instances, reservation/output reconciliation records, timing state, revisions/generators and the process journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 93 exact admission callables. Per-callable contract and test anchors are in _goal4_handoff/B03/public_api_anchors.json and docs/EngineFramework/Processes.md.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation and module-specific public dependencies. B03 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] Input and output providers are transactional ports. Prepare, commit, cancel, compensation and retry/reconciliation paths are part of the local contract. Four admission external-boundary candidates were reviewed. Callback/provider failures are converted to Result/error semantics; durable reconciliation is retained where rollback can fail.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns process definitions, recipes, stations, process instances, reservation/output reconciliation records, timing state, revisions/generators and the process journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Secondary indexes, prepared/pending records and bounded journal state were reviewed against primary records; failure paths do not intentionally publish partial index state.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] ID generators, revision and journal sequence/cursor state are persistent contract state where exposed. Publication is staged so failed operations do not consume IDs or revisions.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle candidates were reviewed for allowed, forbidden, terminal, cancel/release/consume/reconciliation and retry behavior as applicable to the module.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative persistent records and required generator/revision/journal continuity. Provider/runtime handles that are only transient are not promoted to reusable semantic identity.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates and stages candidate state before publication; B03 private restore fault seams verify failed staging leaves the live state and persistent counters unchanged where allocation evidence is required.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] No stronger internal thread-safety guarantee is asserted. B03 local correctness assumes externally serialized/owner-thread access; engine-wide concurrency qualification remains Goal 6.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 88 generated mutation obligations across success, no-op, preconditions/invalid input and failure semantics in coverage_reviews.json.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were reviewed against authoritative state, stale/invalid identities and deterministic index-derived results in the registered module suite.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, secondary indexes, reservation/transaction state and revision/journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Capacity/quantity/time/counter bounds and journal retention are reviewed; integer overflow and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit failure boundaries.

### EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Simulation

<!-- module: EngineFramework/GameplayWorldStateOwners/Simulation -->

Target: `EpidemicGameFrameworkSimulation`.

- **Responsibility:** [REVIEWED] Owns Framework simulation regions, layer definitions/executor bindings, resumable interval/task state, completed summaries, task/summary generators, revisions and the bounded simulation journal.

- **Public headers and types:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 45 exact callable rows. Contract/test anchors are in `_goal4_handoff/B05/public_api_anchors.json` and `docs/EngineFramework/Simulation.md`.

- **Dependency list:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed current Framework/Foundation public dependencies. B05 introduces no production dependency on another Bxx production directory.

- **External ports/callbacks/providers/backends:** [REVIEWED] `ISimulationLayerExecutor::Prepare/Commit` is the critical accepted-work boundary. Stable task IDs and idempotent commit remain required. Local terminal publication is now staged before external Commit, and after accepted work only no-throw local state/journal publication remains.

- **Authoritative state:** [REVIEWED] Owns Framework simulation regions, layer definitions/executor bindings, resumable interval/task state, completed summaries, task/summary generators, revisions and the bounded simulation journal.

- **Derived/cache/index state:** [REVIEWED] Derived indexes and bounded journal/read models were reviewed with primary state. Fault paths may not publish a mismatched derived view.

- **ID spaces, generations, revisions and cursors:** [REVIEWED] Generators, revisions and change sequence/cursor state are explicit contract boundaries. Exhaustion is rejected before publication and restore validates continuity.

- **State machines:** [REVIEWED] Lifecycle states, allowed transitions, duplicate/terminal/retry behavior and wrong-lifecycle failures were reviewed against module tests.

- **Persistent and transient state:** [REVIEWED] Snapshot state contains authoritative semantic records and continuity state required by the module. Transient provider/executor bindings are not reused as persistent semantic identities.

- **Snapshot/restore contract:** [REVIEWED] Restore validates into off-state and keeps the live service unchanged on validation/allocation failure; successful restore publishes a self-consistent state and advances/reset journal epoch according to contract.

- **Threading contract:** [REVIEWED] Externally serialized/owner-thread for Goal 4 local correctness. No new thread-safety promise is introduced; Goal 6 owns concurrency qualification.

- **Public mutation API:** [REVIEWED] Reviewed 40 mutation obligations across success, no-op, invalid/precondition and failure semantics.

- **Read/query API for invariants:** [REVIEWED] Read/query surfaces were checked against authoritative records and deterministic derived indexes/order; diagnostic state is not promoted to authority.

- **Local invariants:** [REVIEWED] Primary records, indexes, generators, revisions, lifecycle and journal publication were reviewed together, including invalid/stale/duplicate and failure-atomicity paths.

- **Hard limits, budgets and complexity bounds:** [REVIEWED] Numeric bounds, budgets/retention and ID/revision/change-sequence exhaustion are explicit. B05 fixes preserve bounded journal/summary behavior and avoid unchecked signed overflow.
